## Summary

The complete record correctly identifies species-rank placeholder NCBITaxon:2933264, Acidilobaceae archaeon. Its NCBI identity, synonym, carried lineage and code are supported. All 34 GTDB broadMatch mappings and their source-cluster counts are retained without equivalent xrefs; primary rows corroborate the counts. One confirmed minor finding affects the fixed explanatory note: 15 clusters do not also map to another NCBI taxon, contrary to that note. Zero imported strains and the two source attestations otherwise reproduce the declared inputs.

## Scientific And Domain Assessments

### Exact taxon and source lineage

identity: supported. Targets: NCBITaxon:2933264.

NCBITaxon:2933264 is the SPECIES-rank placeholder Acidilobaceae archaeon, not family Acidilobaceae 871007 or unclassified parent 1915610. All nine carried ancestors including root, their labels/ranks, the parent and genetic code 11 match the pinned inventory and current NCBI response. The UBA158 and UBA162 taxa are siblings under 1915610, not descendants of this generic placeholder.

### Name classes and nomenclatural scope

nomenclature: supported. Targets: NCBITaxon:2933264.

Caldisphaeraceae archaeon is an explicit exact synonym in the pinned NCBI inventory and a synonym in the current NCBI response. No mapped LPSN name was found by the ignored-inclusive identifier/label/slug/synonym search of the complete pinned inventory. The record asserts neither ICNP-validity nor type material. GTDB names remain qualified broadMatch mappings and are not promoted to exact synonyms or equivalent xrefs.

### Imported subtree and source assertion units

completeness: supported. Targets: NCBITaxon:2933264.

The complete pinned parent graph has no descendant under 2933264, and exact subtree selection has no BacDive strain or culture-deposit row. Zero imported strains is supported and does not deny the observed genome catalog entries. The one NCBI NAME and 31-GENOME primary GTDB attestation use distinct units. No MediaDive, GOLD, Madin or BactoTraits count is asserted, and the inspected complete count inventories have no exact target row. Every one of the 34 direct GTDB mappings is represented; the 54 selected reverse-mapping rows are context for those clusters, not 54 target mappings.

### Qualified species mapping and target scope

grounding: concern. Targets: NCBITaxon:2933264.

The complete selected RS232 source objects contain 126 genome rows across the 34 represented clusters, and each cluster count matches its exact inventory row. Of these rows, 97 carry target ncbi_taxid 2933264. The primary DSZV01 sp030549835 cluster has 31 rows: 30 target-taxid genomes and representative GB_GCA_030549835.1 with taxid 2056631. The record correctly reports 31 for that selected source cluster and explicitly points to 33 further pooled clusters; 126 and 97 are audit counts, not replacements for its attestation. All source predicates remain broadMatch. The fixed note claiming each cluster also maps to other NCBI taxa is false for 15 clusters, including JAWAWX01 sp038824665 whose 20 members all carry 2933264 and whose only reverse mapping is row 235779. Other clusters do mix NCBI taxa, so the distinction must be preserved. GTDB uses Thermoprotei_A/Sulfolobales while NCBI carries Thermoprotei/Acidilobales; the record correctly retains NCBI lineage.

### Unchanged native status and history

provenance: supported. Targets: NCBITaxon:2933264.

The exact resolved NCBI identifier supports EXACT grounding. SEEDED status and the sole seed event at 2026-09-13T17:54:04Z agree with the latest raw/ATB/StrainInfo extraction time. This review does not append curation history or confer native human-reviewed status.

## Findings

### pooled-note-other-taxa: Generated pooled-mapping note incorrectly requires other NCBI taxa for 15 clusters

minor / open / confirmed; issue key: gtdb-broadmatch-note-other-taxa-2933264.

Every broadMatch entry says the source species also maps to other NCBI taxa. Fifteen of this record's 34 clusters instead have only NCBITaxon:2933264 in the complete mapping inventory, and all selected primary members of those clusters carry that same taxid. For example, GTDB:s__JAWAWX01_sp038824665 has one mapping row (data/raw/gtdb_mappings.tsv line 235779) and 20 primary genomes all at taxid 2933264. Its broadMatch is still justified by the pooling of many GTDB species onto this NCBI placeholder; another NCBI target for this individual cluster is not required. The hardcoded explanatory clause is therefore misleading, while the mapping predicates, source counts and main taxon identity remain intact.

## Recommended Actions And Acceptance Checks

### correct-pooled-mapping-explanation

During separately authorized curation, revise the maintained seeder's broadMatch explanation to describe multiple GTDB species mapping to the NCBI taxon without asserting that every individual cluster maps to additional NCBI taxa. Retain the source predicates, all cluster entries, source-scoped counts and carried NCBI lineage; regenerate only through the guarded repository workflow.

- A canary for JAWAWX01_sp038824665 preserves broadMatch and genome_count 20 without claiming another NCBI target absent from the source inventory.
- A mixed-taxon control preserves DSZV01_sp030549835, its broadMatch predicate and count 31; all 34 mappings and the primary attestation remain unchanged except accurate note wording.
- UBA158/UBA162 species closeMatch synonyms and xrefs remain unchanged; no assembly, culture-deposit or lineage equivalence is introduced.
- After authorized guarded regeneration, run just validate-strict for affected records, just validate-history, just validate-products, just verify-corpus and just qc.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- Primary genome checks use the pinned GTDB RS232 catalog, not a new sequence comparison or species-boundary analysis. The explicitly scanned shards are 0017 and 0018; their manifest and source-order implementation corroborate this archaeal tail, and earlier shards were not independently scanned.
- Zero imported BacDive strains is a bounded inventory result. MAG/isolate identifiers do not establish physical culture deposits, type material or assembly equivalence.
- NCBI and GTDB taxonomic hierarchies are retained as independent source assertions; the carried NCBI order Acidilobales is not rewritten to GTDB Sulfolobales. No experimental mechanism, phenotype or ICNP nomenclatural standing is inferred.
- Reused actual native validation on the unchanged shared base 9b5a8fff5185b38dc5c77bb35235152c81ea7765; the recorded cwd and timestamps identify the original execution. These native corpus/source inputs are unchanged; only new review files differ. This receipt is not a new run on the batch branch and does not validate subsequently saved review bundles.
- No generated record or history was edited; curation actions remain proposed.
- Fresh-base canonical recapture preserved all previously reviewed input hashes; original captures and actual transition receipts remain retained.
- Every new bundle still requires its own validate/save/check recipe; baseline native receipts do not claim these later steps have run.

assessments:
- assessment_id: identity
  area: identity
  topic: Exact taxon and source lineage
  outcome: supported
  summary: NCBITaxon:2933264 is the SPECIES-rank placeholder Acidilobaceae archaeon,
    not family Acidilobaceae 871007 or unclassified parent 1915610. All nine carried
    ancestors including root, their labels/ranks, the parent and genetic code 11 match
    the pinned inventory and current NCBI response. The UBA158 and UBA162 taxa are
    siblings under 1915610, not descendants of this generic placeholder.
  target_ids:
  - NCBITaxon:2933264
  evidence_ids:
  - record
  - taxonomy-inventory
  - ncbi-live
  - scope-row
  - path-row
- assessment_id: nomenclature
  area: nomenclature
  topic: Name classes and nomenclatural scope
  outcome: supported
  summary: Caldisphaeraceae archaeon is an explicit exact synonym in the pinned NCBI
    inventory and a synonym in the current NCBI response. No mapped LPSN name was
    found by the ignored-inclusive identifier/label/slug/synonym search of the complete
    pinned inventory. The record asserts neither ICNP-validity nor type material.
    GTDB names remain qualified broadMatch mappings and are not promoted to exact
    synonyms or equivalent xrefs.
  target_ids:
  - NCBITaxon:2933264
  evidence_ids:
  - record
  - taxonomy-inventory
  - ncbi-live
  - lpsn-inventory
  - mapping-policy
- assessment_id: completeness
  area: completeness
  topic: Imported subtree and source assertion units
  outcome: supported
  summary: The complete pinned parent graph has no descendant under 2933264, and exact
    subtree selection has no BacDive strain or culture-deposit row. Zero imported
    strains is supported and does not deny the observed genome catalog entries. The
    one NCBI NAME and 31-GENOME primary GTDB attestation use distinct units. No MediaDive,
    GOLD, Madin or BactoTraits count is asserted, and the inspected complete count
    inventories have no exact target row. Every one of the 34 direct GTDB mappings
    is represented; the 54 selected reverse-mapping rows are context for those clusters,
    not 54 target mappings.
  target_ids:
  - NCBITaxon:2933264
  evidence_ids:
  - record
  - taxonomy-inventory
  - bacdive-inventory
  - culture-inventory
  - gtdb-species
  - gold-organisms
  - mediadive-taxa
  - madin-taxa
  - bactotraits-taxa
  - genome-catalog-1
  - genome-catalog-2
- assessment_id: taxonomy-mapping
  area: grounding
  topic: Qualified species mapping and target scope
  outcome: concern
  summary: 'The complete selected RS232 source objects contain 126 genome rows across
    the 34 represented clusters, and each cluster count matches its exact inventory
    row. Of these rows, 97 carry target ncbi_taxid 2933264. The primary DSZV01 sp030549835
    cluster has 31 rows: 30 target-taxid genomes and representative GB_GCA_030549835.1
    with taxid 2056631. The record correctly reports 31 for that selected source cluster
    and explicitly points to 33 further pooled clusters; 126 and 97 are audit counts,
    not replacements for its attestation. All source predicates remain broadMatch.
    The fixed note claiming each cluster also maps to other NCBI taxa is false for

    so the distinction must be preserved. GTDB uses Thermoprotei_A/Sulfolobales while
    NCBI carries Thermoprotei/Acidilobales; the record correctly retains NCBI lineage.'
